- **waf-false-positives**: Use when reviewing Google Cloud Armor or HTTPS Load Balancer logs to find WAF false positives — legitimate traffic your security policy is blocking. For tuning over-broad rules, investigating blocked users (by country/ASN), and confirming whether blocks are real customers or attackers. GCP-specific; requires the gcloud CLI. ([bartekbp/claude-skills](https://github.com/bartekbp/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/gcp/skills/waf-false-positives))
- **waf-fingerprint**: Identify whether a WAF/CDN edge sits in front of the target, which vendor it is, and which generic bypass techniques apply to that vendor — via wafw00f and manual response-header/ behavioral analysis. Use right after active-recon confirms liveness and before port-scan/ * skills invest effort assuming they're reaching the real origin. Do NOT use for general technology fingerprinting (framework, CMS, server) — that's tech-stack-fingerprint; this skill is specifically about the WAF/edge layer and whether it is intercepting traffic before reaching the real backend. ([mahumadad/quarry](https://github.com/mahumadad/quarry/tree/HEAD/skills/recon/waf-fingerprint) · ★1)

- **wagents-taste**: Calibrate frontend design taste dials (DESIGN_VARIANCE, MOTION_INTENSITY, VISUAL_DENSITY) for the frontend-designer agent. ([ehadziabdic/WAgents](https://github.com/ehadziabdic/WAgents/tree/HEAD/opencode/skills/wagents-taste))
- **wagents-verify**: Run automated test suites, type checks, linting, and build validation before declaring work complete. ([ehadziabdic/WAgents](https://github.com/ehadziabdic/WAgents/tree/HEAD/opencode/skills/wagents-verify))
- **wagf-coupling-designer**: Walk a researcher through designing the LLM-external-model interface for a WAGF domain using the v0.6 coupling layer. Use when the user says "I want to couple my LLM agents to &lt;my simulator&gt;", "help me design the WAGF interface", "scaffold the external model adapter", "draft a coupling contract", or "I have a Python / R / CSV / service model and want WAGF to drive it". ([WenyuChiou/WAGF](https://github.com/WenyuChiou/WAGF/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/wagf-coupling-designer))
- **wagf-domain-builder**: Walk a researcher (PhD, collaborator, lab-mate) through building their first WAGF domain — from "I have a research question + maybe an external model" to "I have a working WAGF experiment producing audit traces." Conducts a structured S0-S7 interview, invokes `broker.tools.scaffold_domain` at S4, guides 4 surgical edits in S5, and runs `broker.tools.validate_prompt` after every change. Hands off to `wagf-coupling-designer` for coupling and to `wagf-verification-ladder` once the domain runs green. Use when the user says "I want to build a WAGF model for &lt;my domain&gt;", "help me set up a new doma… ([WenyuChiou/WAGF](https://github.com/WenyuChiou/WAGF/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/wagf-domain-builder))
- **wagf-experiment-designer**: Turn a WAGF research question into a reproducible experiment matrix (model × governance × seed × metric × artefact path). Use when the user says "design an experiment", "plan an ablation", "compare strict vs disabled", "set up cross-model evaluation", or wants a runnable matrix written to .research/. ([WenyuChiou/WAGF](https://github.com/WenyuChiou/WAGF/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/wagf-experiment-designer))
- **wagf-quickstart**: First-time WAGF setup walkthrough — environment check, smoke test, first experiment, and handoff to the lifecycle skills. Use when the user says "I just cloned WAGF", "set up WAGF", "first WAGF run", "I'm new to this", "where do I start with WAGF", or opens a Claude Code session in a freshly-cloned WAGF repo without a clear task. ([WenyuChiou/WAGF](https://github.com/WenyuChiou/WAGF/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/wagf-quickstart))
- **wagf-verification-ladder**: Use when a WAGF user asks to test a new domain end to end, run the smoke pipeline, decide whether a domain is done, continue after a domain runs, or verify a WAGF setup before experiment design or reproducibility audit. ([WenyuChiou/WAGF](https://github.com/WenyuChiou/WAGF/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/wagf-verification-ladder))
